- **epci-3-finalize-v2_7**: &gt; EPCI workflow: **Explore → Plan → Code → Inspect** &gt; `epci-3-finalize` is the **final step** of the full EPCI workflow for **STANDARD** and **LARGE** changes. &gt; &gt; This command **inspects the implementation**, **prepares documentation**, and **finalizes the feature**. &gt; It does **NOT** implement new features or perform refactors.
